````text
Output:
# IHES Cube Model Run

MEPhI 8xA100 launcher for `cayleypy-ihes-cube` using a real PyTorch model.

The launcher expects the model at:

```text
/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/model.pth
```

Run `prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh` from the visible `basis` shell before `sbatch`.
It downloads the IHES competition data, downloads the model from the GitHub
release asset when missing, and exports the `.pth` model to
`stream1_weights_ihes_bf16`.

The SLURM `start.sh` does not perform network downloads. It only checks prepared
inputs, compiles the runner with IHES state sizing, and runs
`production_runner` through `torchrun` on 8 GPUs.

Default run is intentionally small:

```text
PUZZLE_ID=0
DEPTH_LIMIT=12
BEAM_WIDTH=30000000
```

## Run

From the visible MEPhI terminal on `basis`:

```bash
cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/repo
git fetch origin main --depth 1
git reset --hard origin/main
git log -1 --oneline

cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
mkdir -p ihes_cube_model
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_cube_model.sh ihes_cube_model/start.sh
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare.sh
sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_cube_model/start.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare.sh
bash -n ihes_cube_model/start.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare.sh
chmod +x ihes_cube_model/start.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare.sh

cd ihes_cube_model
./prepare.sh
sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh
```

Override puzzle, depth, or beam width at submit time:

```bash
sbatch -p kaf12 --export=ALL,PUZZLE_ID=0,DEPTH_LIMIT=20,BEAM_WIDTH=48000000 start.sh
```

Logs:

```bash
tail -f /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/logs/ihes-model-<JOBID>.out
```

## Solution Repair CSV

Prepare per-solution repair inputs with one window size, `K1 + 7` by default:

```bash
cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_solution_repair_single_window.sh ihes_cube_model/
sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_cube_model/prepare_solution_repair_single_window.sh
chmod +x ihes_cube_model/prepare_solution_repair_single_window.sh
ihes_cube_model/prepare_solution_repair_single_window.sh
```

## Fresh bucket array with public result publishing

For one SLURM array task per puzzle, keep `PUZZLE_LIMIT=1`. `PUZZLE_OFFSET` is the first selected puzzle index; each array task adds `SLURM_ARRAY_TASK_ID * PUZZLE_LIMIT`.

```bash
cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/repo
git fetch origin main --depth 1
git reset --hard origin/main
git log -1 --oneline

cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh ihes_cube_model/
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh ihes_cube_model/
sed -i 's/\r$//' ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh
bash -n ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh
chmod +x ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh

PREBUILD_JOB="$(sbatch -p kaf12 --parsable ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh)"
echo "PREBUILD_JOB=${PREBUILD_JOB}"

unset FRESH_RUN_TAG
export KNOWN_LENGTHS="23 24"
export PUZZLE_OFFSET=2
export PUZZLE_LIMIT=1
export BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER="/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_REPO_URL="https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results.git"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_DIR="/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/cayleypy-beam-results"

sbatch -p kaf12 --dependency=afterok:${PREBUILD_JOB} --array=0-80%1 --export=ALL ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh
```

After each puzzle the script writes:

- `data/ihes_cube/puzzles/pXXXX/solutions.tsv`
- `data/ihes_cube/puzzles/pXXXX/unique_solutions.tsv`
- `data/ihes_cube/puzzles/pXXXX/metadata.env`
- `data/ihes_cube/puzzles/pXXXX/summary.md`
- `data/ihes_cube/index.tsv`
- `data/ihes_cube/improvements.tsv`

Publishing is best-effort: local logs remain authoritative if GitHub auth or network is temporarily unavailable.

The array jobs must share BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER; otherwise every puzzle task will rebuild the CUDA binary.

````

```text
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

info = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[1]).read_text())
manifest = json.loads(Path(sys.argv[2]).read_text())
move_count = len(info["generators"])
print(f"prepared_state_len={manifest['state_len']}")
print(f"prepared_num_classes={manifest['num_classes']}")
print(f"prepared_output_dim={manifest['output_dim']}")
print(f"prepared_move_count={move_count}")
if manifest["state_len"] != 72 or manifest["num_classes"] != 72:
    raise SystemExit("IHES manifest dimensions mismatch")
if manifest["output_dim"] != 1:
    raise SystemExit("IHES model must be one-output")
if move_count != 18:
    raise SystemExit("IHES move count mismatch")
PY

echo "prepare_done=1"
```


## 240. Codex

Time: `2026-07-22T18:57:34.860Z` · source line `440`

````text
Да, раньше запускалось не через `kaggle` на `basis`. Рабочий путь был такой: подготовить `model.pth` и `data/` заранее, потом запустить `prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`, prebuild runner, и array через `ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh`.

У тебя упало потому что я дал `kaggle ...` на `basis`, а там `kaggle` не установлен. Правильнее сейчас: скачать/залить модель и `test.csv/puzzle_info.json` заранее, а на `basis` только export/prebuild/sbatch.

**1. На локальной машине PowerShell**
```powershell
$dst = "$env:TEMP\ihes_1001_1002"
New-Item -ItemType Directory -Force $dst | Out-Null

kaggle datasets download -d arabidopsisthalian/model-ihes-1780290207-e40960 `
  -f p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth -p $dst --force

kaggle competitions download -c cayleypy-ihes-cube -f test.csv -p $dst --force
kaggle competitions download -c cayleypy-ihes-cube -f puzzle_info.json -p $dst --force

ssh mephi "ssh basis 'mkdir -p /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/data'"

scp -o ProxyJump=mephi "$dst\p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth" `
  basis:/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/model.pth

scp -o ProxyJump=mephi "$dst\test.csv" `
  basis:/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/data/test.csv

scp -o ProxyJump=mephi "$dst\puzzle_info.json" `
  basis:/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/data/puzzle_info.json
```

**2. На `basis`**
```bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE_DIR=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
REPO_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/repo"
RUN_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/ihes_cube_model"

cd "${BASE_DIR}"

cp "${REPO_DIR}/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh"
cp "${REPO_DIR}/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/"
cp "${REPO_DIR}/hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/"

sed -i 's/\r$//' \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"

bash -n "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"
chmod +x "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"

cd "${RUN_DIR}"
./prepare.sh
```

**3. Submit на 1001 и 1002**
```bash
cat > "${RUN_DIR}/target_solutions_1001_1002.csv" <<'CSV'
puzzle_id,path
1001,f2.r2.-d1.-r2.-d0.-f2.-d2.-f0.-r1.-d0.r1.-f1.d2.r2.f2.d1.-f1.-d2.r0.d2.f0.d1.-d0
1002,r2.d0.f2.-r0.f2.-d1.f2.r2.f1.f0.r2.f2.d2.-r0.d0.-d1.-r2.d1.-f2.d0.-d1.-r1
CSV

PREBUILD_JOB="$(sbatch -p kaf12 --parsable "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh")"
echo "PREBUILD_JOB=${PREBUILD_JOB}"

export SOLUTIONS_CSV="${RUN_DIR}/target_solutions_1001_1002.csv"
export KNOWN_LENGTHS="22 23"
export PUZZLE_OFFSET=0
export PUZZLE_LIMIT=1
export FRESH_RUN_TAG="p1001_1002_m1780290207_20260722"
export BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER="${RUN_DIR}/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_REPO_URL="https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results.git"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/cayleypy-beam-results"

ARRAY_JOB="$(sbatch -p kaf12 --dependency=afterok:${PREBUILD_JOB} --array=0-1%1 --export=ALL \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh")"
echo "ARRAY_JOB=${ARRAY_JOB}"

squeue -u vokirova
```

Это ближе к старому рабочему способу: compute jobs ничего не качают из Kaggle, а работают через подготовленный `model.pth`, `data/`, exported weights и общий prebuilt runner.
````

```text
# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
```

````text
may be referenced only as a path:

```text
~/.ssh/MEPHI/id_rsa_mephi_hpc
```

The `.pub` key may be sent only to MEPhI HPC administrators for access
registration or key replacement. Do not ask the user to type a passphrase into
chat; the user handles passphrase prompts locally in the terminal.

## Built-In Codex Terminal Gateway

Always use the built-in Codex terminal as the single command gateway for MEPhI
HPC work. Do not open, target, paste into, or depend on external `cmd.exe`,
Windows Terminal, or GUI terminal windows for routine cluster work.

Codex may run local preparation, SSH, file transfer, SLURM submission, status
checks, and output reads through the built-in terminal. The assistant must relay
the important command output to the user, including job id, job directory,
status, and `slurm-<id>.out` contents.

When the user needs to enter terminal commands, write those commands in chat as
explicit fenced command blocks. Do not put MEPhI HPC commands into the clipboard,
do not hide them behind clipboard automation, and do not silently paste command
blocks. The user should be able to inspect the exact commands in chat before
running them in the built-in Codex terminal.

If sandboxing substitutes or blocks `ssh`, use the real OpenSSH binary path when
needed:

```text
C:\Windows\System32\OpenSSH\ssh.exe
```

When remote SSH requires unsandboxed execution, request approval for that exact
remote action. Do not work around this by opening an external terminal window.

## Compute Rule

All computations must run through SLURM `sbatch`; never run compute payloads
directly through `ssh`, on `hpc.mephi.ru`, or on login/head/management nodes.

Safe compute pattern:

```bash
cd /mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>
sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh
```

For every compute task:

- use one job directory under `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`;
- create or update a SLURM job script;
- submit with `sbatch`, normally `sbatch -p kaf12 start.sh`;
- capture and report the job id;
- check with `scontrol show job <id>`;
- read output from `slurm-<id>.out`.

If a requested action would compute directly on a login/head node, refuse that
specific action and provide the compliant `sbatch` alternative.

## Partition And Resource Rules

For the known approved workflow, use `basis:kaf12` unless the user explicitly
asks for another farm and the partition policy allows it.

For ordinary CPU applications:

- recommended: `unicluster:cpu`, `basov:cpu`;
- allowed: `cherenkov:cpu`, `tensor:cpu`;
- forbidden for ordinary CPU use: `cherenkov:avx2`, `cherenkov:gpu`,
  `tensor:avx512`, `tensor:gpu`, `basis:gpu`.

GPU partitions are only for applications that really use GPU acceleration.
Choosing a forbidden partition is a serious rules violation.

Choose core/thread counts by scaling tests at `1, 2, 4`. Prefer many small jobs
over one excessively wide job.

## Software And Storage

- Use `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>` for calculation input/output.
- Use `/mnt/pool/3/vokirova` or `/mnt/pool/4/vokirova` for user-installed
  software.
- Do not use the home directory for applications, large datasets, or calculation
  outputs.
- Do not use `sudo`, `su`, or `apt install`.
- Use Python `venv` for all `pip` installs and Python project execution;
  `proot` is allowed.
- Do not rely on internet access from compute nodes. Prepare dependencies before
  compute jobs run: install into a persistent `venv` under `/mnt/pool/3/vokirova`
  or `/mnt/pool/4/vokirova`, or use an offline wheelhouse staged under
  `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`.
- SLURM compute scripts should activate an existing `venv` and run the workload.
  Avoid `pip install` or network downloads inside compute jobs unless they use a
  staged offline wheelhouse.
- HPC storage is not a backup system; important results need external copies.

## Response Behavior

Before proposing or running HPC commands:

1. Route the work through the built-in Codex terminal.
2. Present commands to the user in chat as explicit command blocks; never use
   clipboard insertion for MEPhI HPC commands.
3. If `ssh` is sandbox-substituted or blocked, use
   `C:\Windows\System32\OpenSSH\ssh.exe` with approval when needed.
4. Classify the action as setup, transfer, compile, queue inspection, or
   computation.
5. For computation, convert it into a SLURM job script and `sbatch` submission.
6. Keep jobs under `/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/<jobname>`.
7. Select a compliant partition.
8. Ensure Python commands use a prepared `venv`; compute nodes may have no
   internet.
9. Avoid stdout/stderr redirection for beginners unless there is a clear reason.
10. Always report the job id and output file path after submission.

Known verified test: `job_id=31060`, `partition=kaf12`, `state=COMPLETED`,
`exit=0:0`, output `Hello`.

````

Docker `bash -n` passed for the prepare/start/common scripts, and grep confirmed network download commands are absent from the SLURM script.
```text
655:- Uploaded `cayleypy-ihes-cube.zip` to the same GitHub Release as the IHES model and removed Kaggle CLI usage from `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`. IHES data preparation is now GitHub-only: the prepare script downloads `cayleypy-ihes-cube.zip` from release `ihes-p888-model`, unzips it locally under the job directory, and then exports Stream1 weights. Verification confirmed the release has both assets and `prepare.sh` contains no Kaggle download command.
656:- Added TensorOp alignment padding to the BatchNorm-folded Stream1 exporter. Hidden dimensions are rounded up to multiples of 8 in exported weights/manifests while original dimensions are recorded as `original_hd1/original_hd2`; padded channels use zero weights and zero biases so model outputs are unchanged. The IHES p888 model now exports as `hd1=2560` and `hd2=224` from original `2556/218`. `prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh` now re-exports existing weights when a stale manifest has non-8-aligned hidden sizes.
750:- Added hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh, an IHES bucket workflow that uses solv_uniq.csv only to select puzzle ids with baseline lengths 23,24, solves original states from scratch, stores all bucket solutions, and only runs reflected buckets from actually found original solutions until a shorter-than-baseline solution is found.
751:
752:- Improved hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh resume behavior and reflected-source deduplication. Reflected sources are now deduplicated by the reflected state hash, not just by identical path text; stable FRESH_RUN_TAG output/progress files prevent result truncation on restart; original and reflected done markers let a restarted job continue the current puzzle instead of re-running completed sources.
753:
754:- Added optional public results publishing to hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh. When PUBLISH_RESULTS_REPO_URL or an existing PUBLISH_RESULTS_DIR checkout is configured, each completed puzzle writes per-puzzle solutions.tsv, unique_solutions.tsv, metadata.env, summary.md, plus global IHES index/improvements TSV files and commits/pushes them to the separate results repository. The solver/CUDA architecture is unchanged; publishing is best-effort so completed local logs are retained if GitHub auth or network fails. Verification note: test_results/ihes_bucket_public_results_publish_2026-06-26.md.
755:
756:- Updated IHES fresh bucket array documentation to use one shared precompiled production_runner. The documented flow submits prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh first, then submits the puzzle array with --dependency=afterok:<prebuild_job> and BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner so each one-puzzle task reuses the same binary instead of rebuilding.
```

## 266. Codex

Time: `2026-07-22T18:59:56.213Z` · source line `490`

````text
Да, нашел точную старую схему. Она была **не через Kaggle CLI на `basis`**, а через GitHub release:

- `prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh` качает `cayleypy-ihes-cube.zip` и модель из GitHub release `ihes-p888-model`;
- потом экспортирует веса;
- потом `prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh` собирает один общий runner;
- потом `ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh` запускается array-job с `BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER`.

Я проверил текущий GitHub release `ihes-p888-model`: там сейчас есть только:
- `cayleypy-ihes-cube.zip`
- `p888-t000_1778521793_e32692.pth`

Новой модели `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` там **нет**. Если запускать ровно старым способом, он возьмет старую модель. Команда ниже именно старый GitHub-release workflow.

```bash
ssh mephi
ssh basis
```

```bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE_DIR=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
REPO_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/repo"
RUN_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/ihes_cube_model"

cd "${REPO_DIR}"
git fetch origin main --depth 1
git reset --hard origin/main
git log -1 --oneline

cd "${BASE_DIR}"
mkdir -p "${RUN_DIR}"

cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh"
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh "${RUN_DIR}/"
cp repo/hpc/ihes_cube_model/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh "${RUN_DIR}/"

sed -i 's/\r$//' \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"

bash -n \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"

chmod +x \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh" \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh"

cd "${RUN_DIR}"
./prepare.sh
```

Потом запуск только `1001` и `1002`:

```bash
cat > "${RUN_DIR}/target_solutions_1001_1002.csv" <<'CSV'
puzzle_id,path
1001,f2.r2.-d1.-r2.-d0.-f2.-d2.-f0.-r1.-d0.r1.-f1.d2.r2.f2.d1.-f1.-d2.r0.d2.f0.d1.-d0
1002,r2.d0.f2.-r0.f2.-d1.f2.r2.f1.f0.r2.f2.d2.-r0.d0.-d1.-r2.d1.-f2.d0.-d1.-r1
CSV

PREBUILD_JOB="$(sbatch -p kaf12 --parsable "${RUN_DIR}/prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh")"
echo "PREBUILD_JOB=${PREBUILD_JOB}"

export SOLUTIONS_CSV="${RUN_DIR}/target_solutions_1001_1002.csv"
export KNOWN_LENGTHS="22 23"
export PUZZLE_OFFSET=0
export PUZZLE_LIMIT=1
export FRESH_RUN_TAG="p1001_1002_github_release_20260722"
export BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER="${RUN_DIR}/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_REPO_URL="https://github.com/TryDotAtwo/cayleypy-beam-results.git"
export PUBLISH_RESULTS_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/cayleypy-beam-results"

ARRAY_JOB="$(sbatch -p kaf12 --dependency=afterok:${PREBUILD_JOB} --array=0-1%1 --export=ALL \
  "${RUN_DIR}/ihes_solve_bucket_from_scratch.sh")"
echo "ARRAY_JOB=${ARRAY_JOB}"

squeue -u vokirova
```

Если хочешь именно новую модель `1780290207_e40960`, ее надо сначала добавить в GitHub release или положить как `${RUN_DIR}/model.pth` перед `./prepare.sh`; иначе старый workflow честно запустит старый release checkpoint.
````

```text
# Project Agent Rules

## Operating Rule
- Read `AGENTS.md` before project edits.
- Update `memory/CHANGELOG.md` after meaningful code, config, or architecture changes.
- Update `memory/PROMPTS.md` with user prompts that define requirements.
- Store test outputs, logs, and verification notes under `test_results/`.
- Prefer agent-centered code: explicit contracts, deterministic tests, small files, low hidden state.

## Architecture Source
- Primary architecture contract: `ARCHITECTURE_NEED.md`.
- Current Stream 4 implementation rule: use threshold + compact + CUB/fixed-temp sort/reduce + compact; do not use Stream 4 hash-table dedup.

## Implementation Guardrails
- `State128` logical bytes are `v[0..119]`.
- `State128` padding bytes are `v[120..127]`.
- Persistent frontier padding must be zero.
- `FinalResponse` may temporarily store `target_local_idx` in `v[120..123]`.
- `Hash128` is one logical 128-bit key stored as `{lo, hi}`.
- `CandidateMeta` must remain 32 bytes and 32-byte aligned.
- Stream 3 payload id must be original candidate id, not compact index.
- Stream 4 must not apply shard top-k or semantic shard cap.
```

```text
This plugin is intentionally hybrid:

- Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for repository, issue, pull request, comment, label, reaction, and PR creation workflows.
- Use local `git` and `gh` only when the connector does not cover the job well, especially for current-branch PR discovery, branch creation, commit and push, `gh auth status`, and GitHub Actions log inspection.
- Keep connector state and local checkout context aligned. If the request is about the current branch, resolve the local repo and branch before acting.

Once the intent is clear, route to the specialist skill immediately and do not keep broad GitHub triage in scope longer than needed.

## Connector-First Responsibilities

Handle these directly in this skill when the request does not need a narrower specialist workflow:

- repository orientation once the repo, PR, issue, or local checkout is identified
- recent PR or issue triage
- PR metadata summaries
- PR patch inspection
- PR comments, labels, and reactions
- issue lookup and summarization
- PR creation after a branch is already pushed

Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for those flows because it provides structured PR, issue, and review-adjacent data without depending on a local checkout. If the repository is not already identifiable from the user request or local git context, ask for the repo instead of pretending there is a repo-search flow that may not exist.

## Routing Rules

1. Resolve the operating context first:
   - If the user provides a repository, PR number, issue number, or URL, use that.
   - If the request is about "this branch" or "the current PR", resolve local git context and use `gh` only as needed to discover the branch PR.
   - If the repository is still ambiguous after local inspection, ask for the repo identifier.
2. Classify the request before taking action:
   - `repo or PR triage`: summarize PRs, issues, patches, comments, labels, reactions, or repository state
   - `review follow-up`: unresolved review threads, requested changes, or inline review feedback
   - `CI debugging`: failing checks, Actions logs, or CI root-cause analysis
   - `publish changes`: create or switch branches, stage changes, commit, push, and open a draft PR
3. Route to the specialist skill as soon as the category is clear:
   - Review comments and requested changes: `../gh-address-comments/SKILL.md`
   - Failing GitHub Actions checks: `../gh-fix-ci/SKILL.md`
   - Commit, push, and open PR: `../yeet/SKILL.md`
4. Keep the hybrid model consistent after routing:
   - connector first for PR and issue data
   - local `git` and `gh` only for the specific gaps the connector does not cover

## Default Workflow

1. Resolve repository and item scope.
2. Gather structured PR or issue context through the GitHub app from this plugin.
3. Decide whether the task stays in connector-backed triage or needs a specialist skill.
4. Route immediately when the work becomes review follow-up, CI debugging, or publish workflow.
5. End with a clear summary of what was inspected, what changed, and what remains.

## Output Expectations

- For triage requests, return a concise summary of the repository, PR, or issue state and the next likely action.
- For mixed requests, tell the user which specialist path you are taking and why.
- For connector-backed write actions, restate the exact PR, issue, label, or reaction target before applying the change.
- Never imply that GitHub Actions logs are available through the connector alone. That remains a `gh` workflow.

## Examples

- "Use GitHub to summarize the open PRs in this repo and tell me what needs attention."
- "Help with this PR."
- "Review the latest comments on PR 482 and tell me what is actionable."
- "Debug the failing checks on this branch."
- "Commit these changes, push them, and open a draft PR."

```

```text
---

# Work With Kaggle

Use this skill whenever the task involves Kaggle notebooks, kernels, datasets, or GPU validation runs.

## Project Memory First

For project repositories, read local project guidance before changing notebooks or source:

- `AGENTS.md`
- `memory/PROMPTS.md` and `memory/CHANGELOG.md`, especially recent Kaggle entries
- relevant `test_results/kaggle_*` reports for the same failure/config family

This project has many Kaggle-specific decisions preserved in memory. Do not assume a generic Kaggle workflow is correct when project memory says otherwise.

## Safety And Scope

- Do not upload or publish current uncommitted source to a Kaggle dataset unless the user explicitly approves external export.
- Prefer GitHub-backed Kaggle source when the user has selected GitHub as source of truth.
- Do not push a public Kaggle notebook/kernel unless the user explicitly approves publication or public push.
- Do not make C++/CUDA algorithmic changes while diagnosing Kaggle failures unless the user explicitly approves code changes. Config-only notebook changes are acceptable when useful and consistent with the request.
- Do not revert unrelated local changes. Kaggle notebook edits often coexist with dirty source changes.
- For multi-rank validation, do not fake `WORLD_SIZE=2` on one GPU with both ranks on `LOCAL_RANK=0` unless the user explicitly approves that limited smoke. Real multi-rank validation should use real multi-GPU hardware such as Kaggle 2xT4.

## Kaggle CLI Workflow

Prefer Kaggle CLI for authenticated Kaggle work:

- Check metadata before pushing: `kaggle/kernel-metadata.json` or the selected push package metadata.
- Validate notebooks locally before push: JSON parse and Python AST parse for code cells.
- Push with the intended package directory, then monitor with `kaggle kernels status <owner/slug>`.
- Download outputs/logs after completion or failure and store them under `test_results/`.
- If CLI network/proxy behavior is project-specific, inspect existing scripts/config and preserve local proxy-bypass behavior instead of switching tools silently.

When reporting command output to the user, summarize the important status/version/error lines. The user does not see raw command output.

## Notebook Preparation Rules

For solver notebooks in this project:

- Keep a first config cell with the main run parameters such as beam, depth, puzzle range, model weights URL/path, history mode, and torchrun topology.
- Keep build/checkouts and large transient trees under `/tmp` where possible. Use `/kaggle/working` for logs, CSVs, summaries, and final artifacts.
- Add a preflight cell for large runs: compute local/global beam, world size, shard capacity, Stream1/3/4 guards, static history RAM/disk budgets, and free disk under `/tmp` before clone/build/run.
- Clean stale `/tmp/beam_history_*` and configured history disk paths before runs and between puzzles when static-hybrid history is used.
- For long multi-rank runs, stream selected rank logs live while writing full rank logs to disk.
- Final cells should summarize results and produce user-useful CSV/PNG artifacts, for example solved counts and solution-length histograms.

## Torchrun And Multi-GPU Rules

- Torchrun notebooks should launch the existing binary/processes through explicit topology settings, not implicit GPU auto-detection.
- Expose `TORCHRUN_NPROC_PER_NODE`, `TORCHRUN_NNODES`, `TORCHRUN_NODE_RANK`, and rendezvous endpoint/port in config.
- Derive `WORLD_SIZE` from torchrun topology settings, not from `torch.cuda.device_count()` alone.
- For C++ multi-rank runs, torchrun or Python launchers should only start processes. C++ reads `RANK`, `LOCAL_RANK`, and `WORLD_SIZE`, calls `cudaSetDevice(LOCAL_RANK)`, and creates NCCL communicators itself.
- H100 and T4 notebooks may differ by CUDA architecture/build directory and hardware-specific config, but should otherwise stay comparable when the user asks for paired notebooks.

## Monitoring And Stop Rules

- For diagnostic Kaggle runs, if the user has asked Codex to watch logs and no new logs appear for about 120 seconds, stop the run and inspect downloaded logs. This rule came from project memory and applies to diagnostic/watch tasks.
- Do not stop production/no-timeout runs unless the user explicitly asks or the notebook itself has a configured timeout.
- If a run appears hung because stdout is redirected, check rank log files and whether live-rank streaming is enabled before assuming a stall.
- Preserve logs even when stopping: terminate gracefully when possible, then download output files.

## Failure Diagnosis Checklist

After a Kaggle failure, collect and scan:

- Kaggle worker status and kernel version
- notebook stdout tail
- rank logs under the notebook output directory
- result JSONL/CSV summaries
- GPU budget/preflight output
- explicit Kaggle messages such as disk quota failures

Common patterns in this project:

- `KernelWorkerStatus.ERROR` plus "more disk space than is available" usually means disk quota/static history arena pressure.
- `return_code=-9` can indicate host RAM OOM.
- `code=3002` / `STREAM_FATAL_STREAM3_DOUBLE_BUFFER_OVERFLOW` points to Stream3/Stream4/threshold/capacity flow diagnostics.
- `final selected count does not match score phase counts` points to final selection/histogram/exact-count mismatch.
- `cudaGraphInstantiate: out of memory` may require config-only headroom/shard-capacity changes before deeper code work.
- Silent output after build may simply mean rank stdout is redirected and live rank logging is disabled.

Write a short report under `test_results/` for meaningful Kaggle validations, sweeps, failures, or launches.

## Response Style

- Be explicit about whether you changed source code, notebook config only, or only launched/monitored a run.
- Include Kaggle kernel slug/version/status when known.
- Include exact local report/log paths in `test_results/`.
- Keep recommendations tied to evidence from logs, not guesses.
- If the next step requires exporting source or publishing publicly, ask for explicit approval first.

```

```text
---
name: "yeet"
description: "Publish local changes to GitHub by confirming scope, committing intentionally, pushing the branch, and opening a draft PR through the GitHub app from this plugin, with `gh` used only as a fallback where connector coverage is insufficient."
---

# GitHub Publish Changes

## Overview

Use this skill only when the user explicitly wants the full publish flow from the local checkout: branch setup if needed, staging, commit, push, and opening a pull request.

This workflow is hybrid:

- Use local `git` for branch creation, staging, commit, and push.
- Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for pull request creation after the branch is on the remote.
- Use `gh` as a fallback for current-branch PR discovery, auth checks, or PR creation when the connector path cannot infer the repository or head branch cleanly.

## Prerequisites

- Require GitHub CLI `gh`. Check `gh --version`. If missing, ask the user to install `gh` and stop.
- Require authenticated `gh` session. Run `gh auth status`. If not authenticated, ask the user to run `gh auth login` (and re-run `gh auth status`) before continuing.
- Require a local git repository with a clean understanding of which changes belong in the PR.

## Naming conventions

- Branch: `agent/{description}` when starting from main/master/default.
- Commit: `{description}` (terse).
- PR title: `{description}` summarizing the full diff.

## Workflow

1. Confirm intended scope.
   - Run `git status -sb` and inspect the diff before staging.
   - If the working tree contains unrelated changes, do not default to `git add -A`. Ask the user which files belong in the PR.
2. Determine the branch strategy.
   - If on `main`, `master`, or another default branch, create `agent/{description}`.
   - Otherwise stay on the current branch.
3. Stage only the intended changes.
   - Prefer explicit file paths when the worktree is mixed.
   - Use `git add -A` only when the user has confirmed the whole worktree belongs in scope.
4. Commit tersely with the confirmed description.
5. Run the most relevant checks available if they have not already been run.
   - If checks fail due to missing dependencies or tools, install what is needed and rerun once.
6. Push with tracking: `git push -u origin $(git branch --show-current)`.
7. Open a draft PR.
   - Prefer the GitHub app from this plugin for PR creation after the push succeeds.
   - Derive `repository_full_name` from the remote, for example by normalizing `git remote get-url origin` or by using `gh repo view --json nameWithOwner`.
   - Derive `head_branch` from `git branch --show-current`.
   - Derive `base_branch` from the user request when specified; otherwise use the remote default branch, for example via `gh repo view --json defaultBranchRef`.
   - If the branch is being pushed from a fork or the PR target differs from the remote that was just pushed, prefer `gh pr create` fallback because the connector PR creation flow expects one repository target and may not encode cross-repo head semantics cleanly.
   - If connector-based PR creation cannot infer the repository or branch cleanly, fall back to `gh pr create --draft --fill --head $(git branch --show-current)`.
   - Write the PR body to a temp file with real newlines when using CLI fallback so the markdown renders cleanly.
8. Summarize the result with branch name, commit, PR target, validation, and anything the user still needs to confirm.

## Write Safety

- Never stage unrelated user changes silently.
- Never push without confirming scope when the worktree is mixed.
- Default to a draft PR unless the user explicitly asks for a ready-for-review PR.
- If the repository does not appear to be connected to an accessible GitHub remote, stop and explain the blocker before making assumptions.

## PR Body Expectations

The PR description should use real Markdown prose and cover:

- what changed
- why it changed
- the user or developer impact
- the root cause when the PR is a fix
- the checks used to validate it

```

```text
  `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_EXTRA_DEPTHS` more depths, gathers solved buckets from all

  original-facing solution for each variant.

  order.

  runs. In this mode Stream1 model inference is not launched; the captured
  ring-slot graph fills the score ring with zero scores so selection behaves as
```

 # Changelog
 
+## 2026-07-22
+- Updated the IHES GitHub-release preparation path to use
+  `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` as the default model release asset for
+  `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`.
+- Added a `model.pth.release_asset` marker check so the default cluster
+  `model.pth` is re-downloaded and Stream1 weights are re-exported when the
+  configured release asset changes, preventing a stale old checkpoint from
+  being reused silently.
+
+## 2026-06-29
+- Added a separate Stream1 transformer export path for Kaggle
+  `piece_transformer` checkpoints. `tools/export_stream1_transformer.py`
+  detects model dimensions from the checkpoint/metadata/generator files,
+  exports the p900 fast input form (`fast_slot_projected` and
+  `fast_piece_static`), transformer block weights, LayerNorm parameters, and a
+  `backend=piece_transformer` manifest without changing the existing MLP
+  exporter.
+- Added `tools/export_stream1.py`, an auto-detecting Stream1 export entrypoint
+  that selects `piece-transformer`, `resmlp-layernorm`, or
+  `batchnorm-folded` from checkpoint tensor keys while delegating existing MLP
+  formats to the unchanged MLP exporter.
+
 ## 2026-06-20
 - Added `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_MODE` for ordinary solve-to-center runs that keep
   searching after the first hit. Stream2 can now record solved candidates
@@ -743,3 +765,9 @@
 - Updated IHES fresh bucket array documentation to use one shared precompiled production_runner. The documented flow submits prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh first, then submits the puzzle array with --dependency=afterok:<prebuild_job> and BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner so each one-puzzle task reuses the same binary instead of rebuilding.
 
 - Fixed IHES live results publishing index generation after raw/backfill metadata added extra keys. The fresh bucket publisher now includes variants/source_files columns in data/ihes_cube/index.tsv and filters metadata rows to declared fields before writing index/improvements, so older per-puzzle metadata cannot crash publishing.
+
+- Added a Superpowers implementation plan for a separate Stream1
+  `piece_transformer` backend. The plan preserves the existing MLP path, uses
+  automatic transformer manifest generation, requires CUDA correctness against
+  PyTorch reference outputs before dispatcher wiring, and ends with a small
+  2xT4 Kaggle smoke run.

```text
 # Changelog
 
+## 2026-07-22
+- Updated the IHES GitHub-release preparation path to use
+  `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` as the default model release asset for
+  `hpc/ihes_cube_model/prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`.
+- Added a `model.pth.release_asset` marker check so the default cluster
+  `model.pth` is re-downloaded and Stream1 weights are re-exported when the
+  configured release asset changes, preventing a stale old checkpoint from
+  being reused silently.
+
+## 2026-06-29
+- Added a separate Stream1 transformer export path for Kaggle
+  `piece_transformer` checkpoints. `tools/export_stream1_transformer.py`
+  detects model dimensions from the checkpoint/metadata/generator files,
+  exports the p900 fast input form (`fast_slot_projected` and
+  `fast_piece_static`), transformer block weights, LayerNorm parameters, and a
+  `backend=piece_transformer` manifest without changing the existing MLP
+  exporter.
+- Added `tools/export_stream1.py`, an auto-detecting Stream1 export entrypoint
+  that selects `piece-transformer`, `resmlp-layernorm`, or
+  `batchnorm-folded` from checkpoint tensor keys while delegating existing MLP
+  formats to the unchanged MLP exporter.
+
 ## 2026-06-20
 - Added `BEAM_SOLVE_BUCKET_MODE` for ordinary solve-to-center runs that keep
   searching after the first hit. Stream2 can now record solved candidates
@@ -743,3 +765,9 @@
 - Updated IHES fresh bucket array documentation to use one shared precompiled production_runner. The documented flow submits prepare_ihes_prebuilt_runner.sh first, then submits the puzzle array with --dependency=afterok:<prebuild_job> and BEAM_PREBUILT_RUNNER=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100/ihes_cube_model/prebuilt-a100-ihes/production_runner so each one-puzzle task reuses the same binary instead of rebuilding.
 
 - Fixed IHES live results publishing index generation after raw/backfill metadata added extra keys. The fresh bucket publisher now includes variants/source_files columns in data/ihes_cube/index.tsv and filters metadata rows to declared fields before writing index/improvements, so older per-puzzle metadata cannot crash publishing.
+
+- Added a Superpowers implementation plan for a separate Stream1
+  `piece_transformer` backend. The plan preserves the existing MLP path, uses
+  automatic transformer manifest generation, requires CUDA correctness against
+  PyTorch reference outputs before dispatcher wiring, and ends with a small
+  2xT4 Kaggle smoke run.
```

## 413. Codex

Time: `2026-07-22T19:10:33.954Z` · source line `708`

````text
Готово. Я сделал старый GitHub-release workflow рабочим для новой модели:

- залил `p888-t000_1780290207_e40960.pth` в GitHub release `ihes-p888-model`;
- обновил `prepare_ihes_cube_model.sh`, теперь default model asset именно новая модель;
- добавил marker `model.pth.release_asset`, чтобы на кластере старый `model.pth` не переиспользовался молча;
- закоммитил и запушил в `origin/main`: `6f95bd6 Update IHES release model asset`;
- проверил через GitHub API: remote `main` = `6f95bd6bdb32b5f6ef7cca32b96967bce6036503`.

На `basis` теперь запускай старым способом:

```bash
set -euo pipefail

BASE_DIR=/mnt/pool/6/vokirova/beam8a100
REPO_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/repo"
RUN_DIR="${BASE_DIR}/ihes_cube_model"

cd "${REPO_DIR}"
git fetch origin main --depth 1
git reset --hard origin/main
git log -1 --oneline
````
